### SkipZFP over the fastest baseline

The interval picks the fastest baseline again in every resample, so it also covers which baseline is fastest. P(faster) is the chance that a random SkipZFP run beats a random run of the baseline fastest by median.

### SkipZFP over the fastest baseline

The interval picks the fastest baseline again in every resample, so it also covers which baseline is fastest. P(faster) is the chance that a random SkipZFP run beats a random run of the baseline fastest by median.

### SkipZFP over the fastest baseline as accurate

runs: 20261027T033029-a677d3

Wall, zone map and payload are wall times on the critical path; with the pipeline they overlap, so they need not add up to the wall time. Planning with the pipeline, and decode always, are summed over work running in parallel, so they can exceed the wall time and must not be stacked.

## exp2 Basilisk u query steps

runs: 20261026T213752-bd097a

Wall, zone map and payload are wall times on the critical path; with the pipeline they overlap, so they need not add up to the wall time. Planning with the pipeline, and decode always, are summed over work running in parallel, so they can exceed the wall time and must not be stacked.
